\displaystyle=\E_{\begin{subarray}{c}P\in\{I,X,Y,Z\}^{\otimes n}\setminus\{I^{\otimes n}\}\end{subarray}}\left[\,\sum_{\ell:\mathrm{leaf}}p_{\ell}(\rho_{\mathrm{mm}})\max\left(0,\,\,1-\E_{S\in\{\pm 1\}}\frac{p_{\ell}\left(\rho_{S,P}\right)}{p_{\ell}(\rho_{\mathrm{mm}})}\right)\,\right].

(503)

We lower bound the following term,

\displaystyle\E_{S\in\{\pm 1\}}\frac{p_{\ell}\left(\rho_{S,P}\right)}{p_{\ell}(\rho_{\mathrm{mm}})}

\displaystyle=\E_{S\in\{\pm 1\}}\prod_{t=1}^{N_{\mathrm{C}}}\bra{\psi_{t}}\left(\bigotimes_{\ell=1}^{n}U_{2\ell-1}\right)\left(I+S0.9P\right)\left(\bigotimes_{\ell=1}^{n}U_{2\ell-1}^{-1}\right)\ket{\psi_{t}}

(504)

\displaystyle=\E_{S\in\{\pm 1\}}\prod_{t=1}^{N_{\mathrm{C}}}\left(1+0.9S\bra{\tilde{\psi}_{t}}P\ket{\tilde{\psi}_{t}}\right)

(505)

\displaystyle\geq\exp\left[\sum_{t=1}^{N_{\mathrm{C}}}\E_{S\in\{\pm 1\}}\log\left(1+0.9S\bra{\tilde{\psi}_{t}}P\ket{\tilde{\psi}_{t}}\right)\right]

(506)

\displaystyle=\exp\left[\frac{1}{2}\sum_{t=1}^{N_{\mathrm{C}}}\log\left(1-0.81\bra{\tilde{\psi}_{t}}P\ket{\tilde{\psi}_{t}}^{2}\right)\right]

(507)

\displaystyle=\prod_{t=1}^{N_{\mathrm{C}}}\sqrt{1-0.81\bra{\tilde{\psi}_{t}}P\ket{\tilde{\psi}_{t}}^{2}}.

(508)

The second line is a definition of
\ket{\tilde{\psi}_{t}}=\left(\bigotimes_{\ell=1}^{n}U_{2\ell-1}^{-1}\right)\ket{\psi_{t}}
.
The third line uses Jensen’s inequality.
Hence, we can upper bound the average total variation distance as

\displaystyle\E_{\begin{subarray}{c}P\in\{I,X,Y,Z\}^{\otimes n}\setminus\{I^{\otimes n}\}\end{subarray}}\left[\,\frac{1}{2}\sum_{\ell:\mathrm{leaf}}\left|p_{\ell}(\rho_{\mathrm{mm}})-\E_{S\in\{\pm 1\}}p_{\ell}\left(\rho_{S,P}\right)\right|\,\right]

(509)

\displaystyle\leq\E_{\begin{subarray}{c}P\in\{I,X,Y,Z\}^{\otimes n}\setminus\{I^{\otimes n}\}\end{subarray}}\left[\,\sum_{\ell:\mathrm{leaf}}p_{\ell}(\rho_{\mathrm{mm}})\max\left(0,\,\,1-\prod_{t=1}^{N_{\mathrm{C}}}\sqrt{1-0.81\bra{\tilde{\psi}_{t}}P\ket{\tilde{\psi}_{t}}^{2}}\right)\,\right]

(510)

\displaystyle=\sum_{\ell:\mathrm{leaf}}p_{\ell}(\rho_{\mathrm{mm}})\left(1-\E_{\begin{subarray}{c}P\in\{I,X,Y,Z\}^{\otimes n}\setminus\{I^{\otimes n}\}\end{subarray}}\prod_{t=1}^{N_{\mathrm{C}}}\sqrt{1-0.81\bra{\tilde{\psi}_{t}}P\ket{\tilde{\psi}_{t}}^{2}}\right).

(511)

We can remove the
\max(0,\cdot)
because
1-\prod_{t=1}^{N_{\mathrm{C}}}\sqrt{1-0.81\bra{\tilde{\psi}_{t}}P\ket{\tilde{\psi}_{t}}^{2}}\geq 0
.
We bound the term,

\displaystyle\E_{\begin{subarray}{c}P\in\{I,X,Y,Z\}^{\otimes n}\setminus\{I^{\otimes n}\}\end{subarray}}\prod_{t=1}^{N_{\mathrm{C}}}\sqrt{1-0.81\bra{\tilde{\psi}_{t}}P\ket{\tilde{\psi}_{t}}^{2}}

(512)

\displaystyle\geq\exp\left[\frac{1}{2}\sum_{t=1}^{N_{\mathrm{C}}}\E_{\begin{subarray}{c}P\in\{I,X,Y,Z\}^{\otimes n}\setminus\{I^{\otimes n}\}\end{subarray}}\log\left(1-0.81\bra{\tilde{\psi}_{t}}P\ket{\tilde{\psi}_{t}}^{2}\right)\right]

(513)

\displaystyle\geq\exp\left[-1.215\sum_{t=1}^{N_{\mathrm{C}}}\E_{\begin{subarray}{c}P\in\{I,X,Y,Z\}^{\otimes n}\setminus\{I^{\otimes n}\}\end{subarray}}\bra{\tilde{\psi}_{t}}P\ket{\tilde{\psi}_{t}}^{2}\right]

(514)

\displaystyle\geq 1-1.215\sum_{t=1}^{N_{\mathrm{C}}}\E_{\begin{subarray}{c}P\in\{I,X,Y,Z\}^{\otimes n}\setminus\{I^{\otimes n}\}\end{subarray}}\bra{\tilde{\psi}_{t}}P\ket{\tilde{\psi}_{t}}^{2}

(515)

\displaystyle=1-1.215\sum_{t=1}^{N_{\mathrm{C}}}\frac{1}{2^{n}+1}=1-\frac{1.215N_{\mathrm{C}}}{2^{n}+1}.

(516)

The second line uses Jensen’s inequality.
The third line uses
\log(1-x)\geq-3x,\forall x\in[0,0.94]
.
The fourth line uses
\exp(x)\geq 1+x,\forall x\in\mathbb{R}
.
The last line uses

\E_{\begin{subarray}{c}P\in\{I,X,Y,Z\}^{\otimes n}\setminus\{I^{\otimes n}\}\end{subarray}}P\otimes P=\frac{2^{n}\mathrm{SWAP}-I\otimes I}{4^{n}-1},

(517)

hence
\E_{\begin{subarray}{c}P\in\{I,X,Y,Z\}^{\otimes n}\setminus\{I^{\otimes n}\}\end{subarray}}\bra{\tilde{\psi}_{t}}P\ket{\tilde{\psi}_{t}}^{2}=\frac{2^{n}-1}{4^{n}-1}=\frac{1}{2^{n}+1}
.
Combining with Eq. (
500
), we have

\displaystyle\Omega(1)=

\displaystyle\E_{\begin{subarray}{c}P\in\{I,X,Y,Z\}^{\otimes n}\setminus\{I^{\otimes n}\}\end{subarray}}\left[\,\frac{1}{2}\sum_{\ell:\mathrm{leaf}}\left|p_{\ell}(\rho_{\mathrm{mm}})-\E_{S\in\{\pm 1\}}p_{\ell}\left(\rho_{S,P}\right)\right|\,\right]

(518)

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{\ell:\mathrm{leaf}}p_{\ell}(\rho_{\mathrm{mm}})\frac{1.215N_{\mathrm{C}}}{2^{n}+1}=\frac{1.215N_{\mathrm{C}}}{2^{n}+1}.

(519)

Thus we arrive at the desired lower bound,

N_{\mathrm{C}}=\Omega(2^{n}),

(520)

which concludes the proof of Theorem
L.3
.
